    10→import { getPostUrl } from '@/lib/urls';
    11→
    12→const PAGE_SIZE = siteConfig.pagination.series;
    13→
    14→export async function generateStaticParams() {
    15→  const allSeries = getAllSeries();
    16→  const slugs = Object.keys(allSeries);
    17→  if (slugs.length === 0) return [{ slug: '_' }];
    18→  return slugs.map((slug) => ({ slug }));
    19→}
    20→
    21→export const dynamicParams = false;
    22→
    23→export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }): Promise<Metadata> {
    24→  const { slug: rawSlug } = await params;
    25→  const slug = decodeURIComponent(rawSlug);
    26→  const seriesData = getSeriesData(slug);
    27→  
    28→  if (!seriesData) {
    29→    // If no explicit series metadata, try to infer from posts or return default
    30→    const posts = getSeriesPosts(slug);
    31→    if (posts.length > 0) {
    32→        return {
    33→            title: `${slug} - ${t('series')} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    34→            description: `${posts.length} ${t('posts').toLowerCase()} - ${slug}.`,
    35→        }
    36→    }
    37→    return { title: 'Series Not Found' };
    38→  }
    39→
    40→  const ogImage = seriesData.coverImage && !seriesData.coverImage.startsWith('text:') && !seriesData.coverImage.startsWith('./')
    41→    ? seriesData.coverImage
    42→    : siteConfig.ogImage;
    43→
    44→  return {
    45→    title: `${seriesData.title} - ${t('series')} | ${resolveLocale(siteConfig.title)}`,
    46→    description: seriesData.excerpt,
    47→    openGraph: {
    48→      title: seriesData.title,
    49→      description: seriesData.excerpt,
    50→      type: 'website',
    51→      url: `${siteConfig.baseUrl}/series/${slug}`,
    52→      siteName: resolveLocale(siteConfig.title),
    53→      images: [{ url: ogImage, width: 1200, height: 630, alt: seriesData.title }],
    54→    },
    55→    twitter: {
    56→      card: ogImage !== siteConfig.ogImage ? 'summary_large_image' : 'summary',
    57→      title: seriesData.title,
    58→      description: seriesData.excerpt,
    59→      images: [ogImage],
    60→    },
    61→  };
    62→}
    63→
    64→export default async function SeriesPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
    65→  const { slug: rawSlug } = await params;
    66→  const slug = decodeURIComponent(rawSlug);
    67→  const seriesData = getSeriesData(slug);
    68→  const allPosts = getSeriesPosts(slug);
    69→
    70→  if ((!seriesData && allPosts.length === 0) || (process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production' && seriesData?.draft)) {
    71→    notFound();
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  const page = 1;
    75→  const totalPages = Math.ceil(allPosts.length / PAGE_SIZE);
    76→  const posts = allPosts.slice(0, PAGE_SIZE);
    77→
    78→  // Fallback title if seriesData not found (e.g. no index.md but posts exist via frontmatter)
    79→  const title = seriesData?.title || slug.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + slug.slice(1);
    80→  const description = seriesData?.excerpt;
    81→  const coverImage = seriesData?.coverImage;
    82→  const authors = resolveSeriesAuthors(slug, allPosts);
    83→
    84→  return (
    85→    <div className="layout-main">
    86→      <header className="mb-16">
    87→        {/* Cover image hero */}
    88→        {coverImage && (

    90→            <CoverImage
    91→              src={coverImage}
    92→              title={title}
    93→              slug={slug}
    94→              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
    95→            />
    96→            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-background/60 to-transparent" />
    97→          </div>
    98→        )}
    99→
   100→        <div className="text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto">
   101→          <span className="badge-accent mb-4">
   102→            {t('series')} • {allPosts.length} {t('parts')}
   103→          </span>
   104→          <h1 className="page-title mb-4">
   105→            {title}
   106→          </h1>
   107→          {description && (
   108→            <p className="text-lg text-muted font-serif italic leading-relaxed">
   109→              {description}
   110→            </p>
   111→          )}
   112→          {allPosts.length > 0 && (() => {
   113→            // Pick the first installment respecting sort order:
   114→            // date-desc (default) → oldest is last; date-asc/manual → oldest/first is [0]
   115→            const firstPost = (seriesData?.sort === 'date-asc' || seriesData?.sort === 'manual')
   116→              ? allPosts[0]
   117→              : allPosts[allPosts.length - 1];
   118→            return (
   119→            <Link
   120→              href={getPostUrl(firstPost)}
   121→              className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-full bg-accent text-white text-sm font-medium hover:bg-accent/90 transition-colors no-underline"
   122→            >
   123→              {t('start_reading')}
   124→              <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true">
   125→                <polyline points="9 18 15 12 9 6" />
   126→              </svg>
   127→            </Link>
   128→            );
   129→          })()}
   130→          {authors.length > 0 && (
   131→            <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-muted">
   132→              <span className="mr-1">{t('written_by')}</span>
   133→              {authors.map((author, index) => (
   134→                <span key={author}>
   135→                  <Link
   136→                    href={`/authors/${getAuthorSlug(author)}`}
   137→                    className="text-foreground hover:text-accent no-underline transition-colors duration-200"
   138→                  >
   139→                    {author}
   140→                  </Link>
   141→                  {index < authors.length - 1 && <span className="mr-1">,</span>}
   142→                </span>
   143→              ))}
   144→            </p>
   145→          )}
   146→        </div>
   147→      </header>
   148→
   149→      {/* Series Catalog */}
   150→      <SeriesCatalog posts={posts} totalPosts={allPosts.length} />
   151→
   152→      {totalPages > 1 && (
   153→        <div className="mt-12">
   154→          <Pagination currentPage={page} totalPages={totalPages} basePath={`/series/${slug}`} />
   155→        </div>
   156→      )}
   157→    </div>
   158→  );
   159→}

'';\n\n  const processor = unified()\n    .use(remarkParse)\n    .use(remarkGfm)\n    .use(latex ? remarkMath : () => {})\n    .use(remarkRehype, { allowDangerousHtml: true })\n    .use(rehypeRaw)\n    .use(rehypeSlug)\n    .use(rehypeImageMetadata, { slug, cdnBaseUrl })\n    .use(latex ? rehypeKatex : () => {})\n    .use(rehypeStringify, { allowDangerousHtml: true });\n\n  return String(processor.processSync(content));\n}\n"
}

```
     1→import { visit } from 'unist-util-visit';
     2→import sizeOf from 'image-size';
     3→import path from 'path';
     4→import fs from 'fs';
     5→import { Root, Element } from 'hast';
     6→import { getCdnImageUrl } from './image-utils';
     7→
     8→interface Options {
     9→  slug?: string;
    10→  cdnBaseUrl?: string;
    11→}
    12→
    13→export default function rehypeImageMetadata(options: Options) {
    14→  return (tree: Root) => {
    15→    visit(tree, 'element', (node: Element) => {
    16→      if (node.tagName === 'img' && node.properties && typeof node.properties.src === 'string') {
    17→        const src = node.properties.src as string;
    18→        
    19→        if (src.startsWith('http')) return;
    20→
    21→        let imagePath = '';
    22→        let publicPath = '';
    23→
    24→        if (src.startsWith('./') && options.slug) {
    25→          // Relative path: slug is the full public-relative base path (e.g. posts/my-post, books/my-book, flows/2026/01/15)
    26→          const relative = src.substring(2);
    27→          // Use path.resolve to create absolute path without explicitly invoking process.cwd() in a way that triggers broad matching warnings
    28→          imagePath = path.resolve('public', options.slug, relative);
    29→          publicPath = `/${options.slug}/${relative}`;
    30→        } else if (src.startsWith('/')) {
    31→          // Absolute path from public
    32→          // Remove leading slash for path.resolve
    33→          imagePath = path.resolve('public', src.substring(1));
    34→          publicPath = src;
    35→        } else {
    36→          return;
    37→        }
    38→
    39→        // Always resolve to the public path (and apply CDN prefix if configured).
    40→        // Path resolution must happen regardless of whether the file exists locally,
    41→        // so that images hosted only on CDN still get the correct URL.
    42→        node.properties.src = getCdnImageUrl(publicPath, options.cdnBaseUrl ?? '');
    43→
    44→        // Enrich with dimensions only when the file is available locally
    45→        try {
    46→          if (imagePath && fs.existsSync(imagePath)) {
    47→            const buffer = fs.readFileSync(imagePath);
    48→            const dimensions = sizeOf(buffer);
    49→            if (dimensions) {
    50→              node.properties.width = dimensions.width;
    51→              node.properties.height = dimensions.height;
    52→            }
    53→          }
    54→        } catch {
    55→          // Silently fail
    56→        }
    57→      }
    58→    });
    59→  };
    60→}
    61→
